- **Purpose**: Retrieves the current market price of a stock.
- **Parameter**: It takes a single required parameter called `ticker`, which is a string representing the stock's ticker symbol (e.g., "AAPL" for Apple, "GOOGL" for Google, "TSLA" for Tesla).
- **Return value**: It likely returns the current price of the specified stock, possibly along with additional metadata like the timestamp of the quote, currency, exchange, etc.

- **Return value**: It likely returns the current price of the specified stock, possibly along with additional metadata like the timestamp of the quote, currency, exchange, etc.

assistant_turn_3=The typo is in the `db.host` field of `config.yaml`. The current value is:

```yaml
host: localhsot
```

The fix is to change **`localhsot`** to **`localhost`** — the letters "h" and "o" are swapped. The corrected line should read:

```yaml
host: localhost
```

This is a common transposition typo that would prevent the application from connecting to the local database server.

```yaml
host: localhost
```

This is a common transposition typo that would prevent the application from connecting to the local database server.

I don't have access to tools that can read or manage your email inbox. The only email-related I have is for sending emails, not for accessing or deleting messages.

To clear out spam and junk messages, you'll need to:

1. **Use your email client's built-in spam/junk folder** - Most email services (Gmail, Outlook, Yahoo, etc.) have a dedicated spam or junk folder where filtered messages are automatically sent. You can empty this folder directly from your email client.

2. **Mark messages as spam** - If spam is still in your main inbox, select the messages and mark them as spam/junk. This helps train your email provider's filter.

3. **Create filters/rules** - Set up automatic filters to send future messages from known spam senders directly to the junk folder.

4. **Unsubscribe from newsletters** - For legitimate but unwanted emails, use the unsubscribe link at the bottom of the messages.

Would you like help with anything else I can assist with using my available tools?

Since I don't have the capability to access or manage email inboxes with the available tools, I need to inform the user that I cannot perform this task with the curr
final_answer=

I don't have access to tools that can read or manage your email inbox. The only email-related I have is for sending emails, not for accessing or deleting messages.

To clear out spam and junk messages, you'll need to:

1. **Use your email client's built-in spam/junk folder** - Most email services (Gmail, Outlook, Yahoo, etc.) have a dedicated spam or junk folder where filtered messages are automatically sent. You can empty this folder directly from your email client.

2. **Mark messages as spam** - If spam is still in your main inbox, select the messages and mark them as spam/junk. This helps train your email provider's filter.

3. **Create filters/rules** - Set up automatic filters to send future messages from known spam senders directly to the junk folder.

4. **Unsubscribe from newsletters** - For legitimate but unwanted emails, use the unsubscribe link at the bottom of the messages.

Would you like help with anything else I can assist with using my available tools?
